## Best date plans

### 2026-11-16 — Union Square Holiday Market — Monday browsing + Bar Cosme

**Union Square Holiday Market — Monday browsing, then a meal.** Contemporary Mexican at Bar Cosme. It is a 12-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Union+Square+Park%2C+East+14th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY&destination=11+Great+Jones+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://urbanspacemarkets.com/pages/union-square) · [Restaurant](https://www.instagram.com/barcosmenyc/)

### 2026-11-14 — Roebling Rink opening-day free skate + Azar

**Roebling Rink opening-day free skate, then a meal.** Persian flatbread wraps at Azar. It is 1.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10:30 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Brooklyn+Bridge+Park+beneath+the+Brooklyn+Bridge%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=740+Driggs+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.roeblingrink.com/) · [Restaurant](https://azar.nyc/)

### 2026-11-14 — Brooklyn Flea in DUMBO + Azar

[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=80+Pearl+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&destination=740+Driggs+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.brooklynflea.com/) · [Restaurant](https://azar.nyc/)

Reserve Tickets Become a Member 潮州话、客家话、台语方言角来啦! 🍜 别人搬家是背井离乡，客家人搬家是继承家业；潮汕人搬到哪里，都坚持小孩必须说潮汕话。这次重音方言角 × 纽约美国华人博物馆 MOCA，我们请来四位嘉宾，聊聊台语，潮州话，粤语，和客家话这几个默默称霸海外的方言。我们会一起探索方言里的音调音律，窥探豆腐的历史前生，听听周敦颐第三十六代传人的家族传奇，再跟着潮州博主看看潮汕人在全球扎根的轶事。本次活动我们请来了四位嘉宾: 李岳燁 Dennis：导演、剧作家,也是个夜店咖,常驻DJ台正前方。矿工爺爺带大,家里没人会念「燁」,干脆叫他「华」。曾因为疏於讲台语被外婆训过。 周晓蓉：周敦颐第三十六代传人,汕头金中校友会秘书长。祖先爱莲「出淤泥而不染」,她爱越野,专往泥里钻。 陈洁莹:「潮汕人在纽约」主理人,韩江边长大的野孩子。坚信小孩必须说潮汕话,家里…

## Where to eat

NYC dining options for November 12, 2026: lunch values, recent openings, seasonal menus and Thanksgiving meals. Holiday ordering deadlines and unconfirmed details are noted.

Dining research: 2026-11-12T10:40:47+00:00

No Michelin award claims are retained or added. The reviewed evidence does not establish current Michelin status for these candidates; Michelin's own listings were not checked. Editorial recommendations, guide selection and Michelin stars must not be treated as interchangeable.

### Dynamo's $35.95 express lunch and daily bar happy hour

Deals & happy hours — The Dynamo Room

The express lunch includes rolls, a salad choice and spaghetti pomodoro or salmon. Happy hour offers oysters, smaller martinis and snacks near Penn Station.

Price: Express lunch $35.95; steak frites supplement $10. Happy-hour oysters: 3/$9.95, 6/$18.95 or 12/$36.95; Miller High Life $6.95; wine $12.95; mini dirty martini $11.95.  
Schedule: Lunch 11:30am–2:30pm, Monday–Thursday according to current operating hours. Happy hour daily 4–6pm.  
Conditions: Happy hour is restricted to the bar or terrace. Friday–Sunday opening is 4pm, so lunch should not be assumed available then. Reservation requirements, tax, gratuity and holiday exceptions are not stated.  
[Source](https://v4-thedynamoroom.getbento.com/menus/)

### Amélie's $26 weekday lunch and daily oyster happy hour

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

A $26 two-course weekday lunch and daily oyster happy hour offer separate ways to visit. The happy-hour menu also lists a $26 burger-and-fries or moules-frites offer with beer or wine.

Price: Two-course lunch $26; lunch wine of the day +$10. Happy-hour oysters $1.50 each; six oysters with a glass of wine $19; selected cocktails $10; beer $7; three-wine flight $14. Early bird: burger and fries or moules frites with beer or wine, $26.  
Schedule: Lunch Monday–Friday noon–3:30pm. Happy-hour food daily 4–6pm; drinks from opening until 6pm.  
Conditions: Lunch excludes tax and gratuity; burger supplement $5, steak frites or Niçoise supplement $8. The lunch page specifies two courses despite displaying three course sections. Happy-hour oyster minimum, mandatory reservations and tax/gratuity treatment are unstated. Early-bird offer appears on the happy-hour menu without a separate time window.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/happy-hour)

### Junoon's two- or three-course Indian lunch

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

The Prestige Prix lunch includes basmati rice and naan, with vegetarian, seafood and meat choices such as eggplant chaat, mushroom xacutti and chicken gassi.

Price: Two courses $43; three courses $49.  
Schedule: Lunch; exact days and hours unknown.  
Conditions: The menu states a 20% service charge for parties of five or more in its dinner footer; applicability to lunch was not separately clarified. Tax treatment and mandatory booking are unknown.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

The Fall 2026 luncheon menu lists six $14 pastas, including angel hair Francese, spaghetti anchoiade and ricotta cavatelli alla Norma.

Price: $14 per listed luncheon pasta.  
Schedule: Weekdays 11am–3pm.  
Conditions: Weekday lunch pricing, not a dated special-menu event. Walk-in space is held. Tax, gratuity and minimum purchases are unstated. Do not assume the $14 pasta offer applies on Thanksgiving, for which a separate holiday menu is published.  
[Source](https://www.antonsnyc.com/menus/)

### Miss Lily's $25 brunch drinks add-on

Deals & happy hours — Miss Lily's 7A

Weekend brunch offers unlimited selected drinks with a signature dish purchase, including rum punch and a One Love Bellini.

Price: $25 per person for the drinks add-on, plus a signature dish; examples include $18 coconut pancakes, $20 Jamaican chilaquiles and $23 Jamaican breakfast.  
Schedule: Saturday and Sunday noon–4pm.  
Conditions: A signature dish purchase is required; $25 is the drinks add-on, not the meal total. The linked dinner PDF states that 18% gratuity is automatically added and further tips are optional; its application to brunch is not separately confirmed. Brunch duration, whole-table participation, tax and other fees remain unverified.

Tomer Blechman's Mediterranean restaurant is useful for shared mezze and a planned dinner around Fort Greene, BAM or Downtown Brooklyn. Official dinner hours extend to 10pm Tuesday–Saturday and 9pm Sunday; it is closed Monday. Reservations release 14 days ahead at 9am.

### [Eyval](https://www.eyvalnyc.com/)

Persian · $$$ · Bushwick · Brooklyn · Score 43.0  
Persian breads, dips, tahdig and kebabs make this a substantial Bushwick dinner option. The official menu lists kebabs around $24–$36 and larger braises around $42–$48. Official hours are Tuesday–Saturday 6–10pm and Sunday 5–9:30pm; closed Monday.

### [Cafe Fiorello](https://www.cafefiorello.com/)

Italian · $$$ · Lincoln Square · Manhattan · Score 43.0  
A practical Lincoln Center institution with a vegetable antipasto bar, thin-crust pizzas and pasta. The official menu lists a $39.95 weekday three-course lunch and many pizzas or pastas around $28–$33. Official closing times range from 10pm to 11:30pm, supporting pre- and post-theater planning within those hours.

#### [La Distance — final BAM matinee](https://www.bam.org/theater/2026/la-distance)

2026-11-29 · 3:00 PM · theater · From $25; single-ticket online and phone orders add $11 per ticket. · BAM Harvey Theater · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 69.0  
Tiago Rodrigues's North American premiere follows a daughter who leaves Earth for Mars and the father she leaves behind, performed on a rotating stage by Alison Dechamps and Adama Diop.
**Timing:** Final performance November 29 at 3pm. Listed duration is 85 minutes without intermission; end time is calculated.
**Practical note:** Performed in French with English supertitles.
**Why now:** The final matinee is a concrete deadline for this November visiting production; the alert does not claim tickets are scarce.
**Book soon:** BAM confirms the North American premiere engagement ends November 29, and its official ticket calendar lists the final matinee at 3pm.

2026-11-29 · 3:00 PM · theater · Longacre Theatre · Theater District · Manhattan · Score 65.0  
Sam Tutty and Christiani Pitts star in an original musical comedy about an upbeat British visitor and a New Yorker navigating a wedding, family secrets, and unexpected connection.
**Timing:** Playbill lists the final Sunday performance at 3pm and a duration of 2 hours 15 minutes including intermission. End time is calculated.

**Why now:** The production has announced its actual Broadway closing, not merely the end of a ticket-sales block.
**Book soon:** The production's official website states MUST CLOSE NOVEMBER 29; Playbill supplies the final week's performance schedule.
